\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)^{2}-r_{0}(X)^{2}]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}+4\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle-4\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)^{2}-r_{0}(X)^{2}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}+\iota_{n}^{2})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+4\iota_{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+4\iota_{n}(\bar{\delta}_{n}+\iota_{n})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 16(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)],

provided that
n
is sufficiently large such that
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
. Hence, Theorem
A.6
suggests that, for sufficiently large
n
such that
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
, with
n\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
, and for arbitrary
t>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T_{i})\geq t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T)]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T_{i})\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{t}{2}+\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T)]\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left(\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}:\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,Z)]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(f,Z_{i})\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{t}{2}+\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)]\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 14\mathcal{N}_{n}\left(c_{2}t,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g_{\iota_{n}}(f,\cdot):\mathcal{X}^{t}\times\mathcal{X}^{s}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{nt}{c_{3}(\log n)^{4+4\kappa}}\right),

where
c_{2},c_{3}
are universal constants and
\mathcal{X}^{s},\mathcal{X}^{t}
represents the domain of
X^{s},X^{t}
, respectively. Subsequently, we bound the covering number. Fix
\{x^{s}_{1},\dots,x^{s}_{n}\}\subset(\mathcal{X}^{s})^{n}
and
\{x^{t}_{1},\dots,x^{t}_{n}\}\subset(\mathcal{X}^{t})^{n}
. Let
\mathcal{C}=\{x^{s}_{1},\dots,x^{s}_{n},x^{t}_{1},\dots,x^{t}_{n}\}
, and let
h^{\sharp}=\{h_{1},\dots,h_{k}\}
be an
\epsilon
-covering set of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}|\mathcal{C}}
where
h_{i}=f_{i|\mathcal{C}}
for some
f_{i}\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}(i=1,\dots,k)
, such that for any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
, there exists
h^{*}=f^{*}_{|\mathcal{C}}\in h^{\sharp}
satisfying
\|h^{*}-f_{|\mathcal{C}}\|_{\infty}<\epsilon
. This indicates

\displaystyle|g_{\iota_{n}}(f,(x^{s}_{i},x^{t}_{i}))-g_{\iota_{n}}(f^{*},(x^{s}_{i},x^{t}_{i}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|f(x^{s}_{i})^{2}-f^{*}(x^{s}_{i})^{2}|+2|f(x^{t}_{i})-f^{*}(x^{t}_{i})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\epsilon.

Therefore,

\mathcal{N}_{n}(c_{2}t,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g_{\iota_{n}}(f,\cdot):\mathcal{X}^{t}\times\mathcal{X}^{s}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\})\leq\mathcal{N}_{2n}(c_{2}t/[2(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)],\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}).

Then, with Lemma
A.4
and Lemma
A.5
, for sufficiently large
n
with
n\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
and any
a_{n}\geq 1/n
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{n}+14\int_{a_{n}}^{\infty}\mathcal{N}_{2n}(c_{2}t/[2(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)],\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})\exp\left(-\frac{nt}{c_{3}(\log n)^{4+4\kappa}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{n}+14\mathcal{N}_{2n}(c_{2}a_{n}/[2(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)],\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})\int_{a_{n}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{nt}{c_{3}(\log n)^{4+4\kappa}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{n}+14\left(c_{4}n^{2}\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\right)^{c_{5}SL\log S}\cdot\frac{c_{3}(\log n)^{4+4\kappa}}{n}\exp\left(-\frac{na_{n}}{c_{3}(\log n)^{4+4\kappa}}\right),

where
c_{4}
and
c_{5}
are universal constants. Choose

a_{n}=\frac{c_{3}c_{5}(\log n)^{4+4\kappa}}{n}SL\log S\log\left(c_{4}n^{2}\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\right).

For sufficiently large
n
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}},T_{i})\right\}\leq\frac{c_{6}SL\log S(\log n)^{5+4\kappa}}{n},

where
c_{6}
is a constant not depending on
S,L
and
n
. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.3
.

Assume that

(i)

r_{0}(x)\in\mathcal{H}^{\beta_{r}}_{\mathrm{Loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{d},B_{u})
with
\beta_{r}>0
and
B_{u}\leq c(u^{m}+1)
for some universal constants
c>0
,
m\geq 0
;

(ii)

r_{0}(X^{s})
and
\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}
are sub-exponentially distributed random variables.

Suppose that the depth
L
and width
M
of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
are expressed as

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=21(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}S_{1}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{1})\rceil+2d+3,

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle=38(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1}S_{2}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{2})\rceil,

for any
S_{1},S_{2}\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. Let
\bar{\delta}=\bar{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{1+\kappa}
, with an arbitrarily fixed
\kappa\in(0,1]
, and
\underline{\delta}=0
. Then, for sufficiently large
n
, it follows that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c^{*}\left\{\left[(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}(\log n)^{m}\right]^{2}+\frac{(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}}{n}\right\},

where
c^{*}
is a constant not depending on
S_{1},S_{2}
and
n
.

For any
\iota_{n}>0
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{n})\right\}.

On the one hand, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{f(X)^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{n})\right\}+2\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{r_{0}(X)^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle+\frac{32}{\varsigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle+\frac{32}{\varsigma^{2}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty})]\right\}^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}(\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}+1)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

where
c_{1}
is a constant which depends only on
\varsigma,\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty})]
and
\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]
. On the other hand, we first notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+2\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[f(X)^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right]+2\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[r_{0}(X)^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+2\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)+\frac{32}{\varsigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}+c_{2}(\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}+1)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

where
c_{2}
is a constant which depends only on
\varsigma
and
\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]
. Then, we focus on
\{x:\|x\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n}\}=[-\iota_{n},\iota_{n}]^{d}
. Let
r_{0}^{*}(x)=r_{0}(2\iota_{n}x-\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d})
for
x\in[0,1]^{d}
. Lemma
A.2
demonstrates that for any
S_{1},S_{2}\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, there exists a function
f^{*}
implemented by a ReLU network with depth
L^{*}=21(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}S_{1}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{1})\rceil+2d
, width
M^{*}=38(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1}S_{2}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{2})\rceil
, such that

|f^{*}(x)-r_{0}^{*}(x)|\leq 18c(\iota_{n}^{m}+1)(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d},

for all
x\in[0,1]^{d}\backslash\Omega([0,1]^{d},K,\Delta)
. Here,

\Omega([0,1]^{d},K,\Delta)=\bigcup_{i=1}^{d}\left\{x=(x_{1},\dots,x_{d})^{\top}:x_{i}\in\bigcup_{k=1}^{K-1}(k/K-\Delta,k/K)\right\},

where
K=\lceil(S_{1}S_{2})^{2/d}\rceil
and
\Delta
is an arbitrary scalar in
(0,1/(3K)]
. Let
f^{\dagger}(x)=f^{*}((x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d})/(2\iota_{n}))
for
x\in[-\iota_{n},\iota_{n}]^{d}
. We obtain that

|f^{\dagger}(x)-r_{0}(x)|\leq 18c(\iota_{n}^{m}+1)(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d},

for all
x\in[-\iota_{n},\iota_{n}]^{d}\backslash\Omega^{\dagger}
, where
\Omega^{\dagger}=\{x:(x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d})/(2\iota_{n})\in\Omega([0,1]^{d},K,\Delta)\}
. Furthermore, note that

f^{\dagger}(x)=f^{*}\left(\frac{x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d}}{2\iota_{n}}\right)=f^{*}\left(\mathrm{relu}\left(\frac{x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d}}{2\iota_{n}}\right)-\mathrm{relu}\left(-\frac{x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d}}{2\iota_{n}}\right)\right),

which is implemented by a neural network with ReLU activations, depth
L^{\dagger}=L^{*}+1
, and width
M^{\dagger}=M^{*}
. In addition, let

f^{\ddagger}(x)=\begin{cases}\bar{\delta}_{n},&f^{\dagger}(x)>\bar{\delta}_{n},\\
f^{\dagger}(x),&0\leq f^{\dagger}(x)\leq\bar{\delta}_{n},\\
0,&f^{\dagger}(x)<0.\end{cases}

A straightforward calculation shows that

f^{\ddagger}(x)=\mathrm{relu}(-\mathrm{relu}(-f^{\dagger}(x)+\bar{\delta}_{n})+\bar{\delta}_{n}),

indicating that
f^{\ddagger}(x)
can be implemented by a ReLU network with depth
L=L^{*}+3
and width
M=M^{*}
. Due to the arbitrariness of
\Delta
, when
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f^{\ddagger}(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[18c(\iota_{n}^{m}+1)(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}\right]^{2}.

Recall that
\bar{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{1+\kappa}
, and set
\iota_{n}
to
(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1
. We conclude that for sufficiently large
n
satisfying
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
and
\log n\geq\varsigma/2
, it holds that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f(X)-r_{0}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+(c_{1}+c_{2})(\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}+1)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{3}\left\{\left[(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}(\log n)^{m}\right]^{2}+\frac{(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}}{n}\right\},

where
c_{3}
is a constant not depending on
S_{1},S_{2}
and
n
.
∎

To commence, we notice that Lemma
B.1
, Lemma
B.2
and Lemma
B.3
indicate

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}SL\log S(\log n)^{5+4\kappa}}{n}

\displaystyle+c_{2}\left\{\left[(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}(\log n)^{m}\right]^{2}+\frac{(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}}{n}\right\},

where
c_{1},c_{2}
are constants not depending on
S,L,S_{1},S_{2}
and
n
, and
S_{1},S_{2}
satisfy the conditions that the network depth
L=21(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}S_{1}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{1})\rceil+2d+3
, network width
M=38(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1}S_{2}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{2})\rceil
, for sufficiently large
n
and
n\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
. Therefore, by letting
S_{1}=\mathcal{O}(n^{d/(2d+4\beta_{r})})
and
S_{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, we obtain

M=\mathcal{O}(1),\quad L=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{\frac{d}{2d+4\beta_{r}}}\log n\right),\quad S=\mathcal{O}(M^{2}L)=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{\frac{d}{2d+4\beta_{r}}}\log n\right),

yielding

\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}})\leq c_{3}n^{-\frac{2\beta_{r}}{d+2\beta_{r}}}(\log n)^{(8+4\kappa)\vee(2m)},

where
c_{3}
is a constant not depending on
n
, for
n\geq 2
. Furthermore, observe that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{t}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{t})-r_{0}(X^{t})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\right\}

Similar truncation operation suggests that, for any
\iota_{n}>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{t}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}})=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\iota_{n}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\iota_{n}\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}})+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right\}.

Subsequently, note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}}(X^{s})^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{3}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{3}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\varsigma^{-1}\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X^{s}))]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)+432\varsigma^{-3}\mathbb{E}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X^{s}))]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{4}(\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}+1)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{4}[(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}+1]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

where
c_{4}
is a constant depending only on
\varsigma
and
\mathbb{E}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X^{s}))]
. Hence, by taking
\iota_{n}=(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1
, we obtain that

\mathcal{R}^{t}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}})\leq[(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1]\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LS}})+\frac{c_{4}[(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}+1]}{n}.

This completes the proof.
∎

B.2
Proof of Lemma 3.2

Given
r_{0}\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})
, we first claim that for any function
f\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})
, we have
\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|f(X^{s}))<\infty
. In fact, it follows that

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|f(X^{s}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big\{r_{0}(X)\log r_{0}(X)-[r_{0}(X)+1]\log(r_{0}(X)+1)+\log(f(X)+1)

\displaystyle-r_{0}(X)\log f(X)+r_{0}(X)\log(f(X)+1)\big\}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big\{\left|r_{0}(X)\log r_{0}(X)\right|+[r_{0}(X)+1]\log(r_{0}(X)+1)+\log(f(X)+1)

\displaystyle+\left|r_{0}(X)\log f(X)\right|+r_{0}(X)\log(f(X)+1)\big\}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\Big).

Recall that
\mathbb{E}_{P}h(X)\equiv\mathbb{E}h(X^{s})
for any
X^{s}
-integrable function
h
. Then, observe that for any scalars
x,y\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
, we have
\max(\log x,\log(x+1))\leq x
and
|x\log y|\leq xy^{-1}+xy
. Hence, it holds that

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|f(X^{s}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big\{\left|r_{0}(X)\log r_{0}(X)\right|+[r_{0}(X)+1]\log(r_{0}(X)+1)+\log(f(X)+1)

\displaystyle+\left|r_{0}(X)\log f(X)\right|+r_{0}(X)\log(f(X)+1)\big\}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big\{1+r_{0}(X)^{2}+[r_{0}(X)+1]r_{0}(X)+f(X)

\displaystyle+r_{0}(X)f(X)^{-1}+r_{0}(X)f(X)\big\}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{1+r_{0}(X)^{2}+[r_{0}(X)+1]r_{0}(X)+f(X)

\displaystyle+r_{0}(X)f(X)^{-1}+r_{0}(X)f(X)\big\}<\infty.

Next, note that
\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|r_{0}(X^{s}))=0
. Therefore, for any minimizer
f^{*}
of
\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|f(X^{s}))
with respect to
f\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})
, we have
\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|f^{*}(X^{s}))=0
, which indicates that

D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|f^{*}(X^{s}))=0\text{ a.s. }X^{s}.

Let the set
\mathcal{A}=\{x\in\mathcal{X}^{t}:D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(x)\|f^{*}(x))=0\}
. Notice that
\varphi_{\mathrm{LR}}^{\prime\prime}(x)=[x(x+1)]^{-1}>0
for any
x\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
, thus
\varphi_{\mathrm{LR}}
is strictly convex over
\mathbb{R}_{+}
. By Lemma
A.1
, we obtain that

1=\mathrm{P}(X^{s}\in\mathcal{A})\leq\mathrm{P}(r_{0}(X^{s})=f^{*}(X^{s})).

Consequently, it follows that
r_{0}(X^{s})=f^{*}(X^{s})
a.s.
X^{s}
.
∎

B.3
Proof of Theorem 3.3

For any function
f\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})
, define

\displaystyle J(f)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\log(f(X)+1)-r_{0}(X)\log f(X)+r_{0}(X)\log(f(X)+1)\right],

\displaystyle J_{n}(f)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\log(f(X^{s}_{i})+1)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[-\log f(X^{t}_{i})+\log(f(X^{t}_{i})+1)\right].

Here,
\mathbb{E}_{P}[h(X)]\equiv\mathbb{E}[h(X^{s})]
for any
X^{s}
-integrable function
h
, where the expectation is taken with respect to
X^{s}
. Analogously,
\mathbb{E}_{Q}[h(X)]\equiv\mathbb{E}[h(X^{t})]
for any
X^{t}
-integrable function
h
.

Lemma B.4
.

Assume that
r_{0}(X^{s})
and
r_{0}(X^{s})^{-1}\mathds{1}(X^{s}\in\mathcal{X}^{t})
are square-integrable. Then, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]+2\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X)).

Given that
r_{0}(X^{s})
and
r_{0}(X^{s})^{-1}\mathds{1}(X^{s}\in\mathcal{X}^{t})
are square-integrable, it is evident that
r_{0}\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})
. In addition, setting
\underline{\delta}
to arbitrary positive scalar implies
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\subset\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})
. Subsequently, for any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s}))

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-J(r_{0})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-J(r_{0})]+2\mathbb{E}[J_{n}(f)-J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-J(r_{0})]+2\mathbb{E}[J_{n}(f)-J_{n}(r_{0})+J_{n}(r_{0})-J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]+2[J(f)-J(r_{0})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]+2\mathbb{E}_{P}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X)).

Take the infimum on both sides with respect to
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
and we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma B.5
.

Assume that
r_{0}(X^{s})
and
r_{0}(X^{s})^{-1}\mathds{1}(X^{s}\in\mathcal{X}^{t})
are sub-exponentially distributed random variables. Let
\bar{\delta}=\bar{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{1+\kappa}
and
\underline{\delta}=\underline{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{-1-\kappa}
for arbitrarily fixed constant
\kappa\in(0,1]
. Then, for sufficiently large
n
and
n\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
, it follows that

\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]\leq\frac{c^{*}SL\log S(\log n)^{6+5\kappa}}{n},

where
c^{*}
is a constant not depending on
S,L
and
n
.

Let
T_{i}=(X^{s}_{i},X^{t}_{i})
for
i=1,\dots,n
,
\mathcal{D}_{n}=\{T_{1},\dots,T_{n}\}
, and
T=(X^{s},X^{t})
be an independent copy of
T_{1}
. Firstly, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\{J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-J(r_{0})-2[J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-J_{n}(r_{0})]\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\right\},

where

\displaystyle g(f,T)=

\displaystyle\log(f(X^{s})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{s})+1)

\displaystyle+\log r_{0}(X^{t})-\log f(X^{t})

\displaystyle+\log(f(X^{t})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{t})+1),

for
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
. Next, we introduce a truncation step. For any
\iota_{n}\geq 1
and any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
, define

\displaystyle g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)=

\displaystyle[\log(f(X^{s})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{s})+1)]\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X^{s})\leq\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+[\log r_{0}(X^{t})-\log f(X^{t})]\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X^{t})\leq\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+[\log(f(X^{t})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{t})+1)]\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X^{t})\leq\iota_{n}).

Then, it follows that

\displaystyle|g(f,T)-g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)|\leq

\displaystyle|\log(f(X^{s})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{s})+1)|\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+|\log(f(X^{s})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{s})+1)|\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})<\iota_{n}^{-1})

\displaystyle+|\log r_{0}(X^{t})-\log f(X^{t})|\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+|\log r_{0}(X^{t})-\log f(X^{t})|\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})<\iota_{n}^{-1})

\displaystyle+|\log(f(X^{t})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{t})+1)|\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+|\log(f(X^{t})+1)-\log(r_{0}(X^{t})+1)|\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})<\iota_{n}^{-1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(r_{0}(X^{s})+1)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})<\iota_{n}^{-1})

\displaystyle+[\log r_{0}(X^{t})+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+[-\log r_{0}(X^{t})+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})<\iota_{n}^{-1})

\displaystyle+[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(r_{0}(X^{t})+1)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle+[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{t})<\iota_{n}^{-1}).

Taking expectation, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log r_{0}(X)+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[-\log r_{0}(X)+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}.

Specifically, for
n\geq 3
, observe that firstly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+r_{0}(X)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)+\frac{2}{\varsigma}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))\right\}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

secondly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathbb{E}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

thirdly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log r_{0}(X)+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[r_{0}(X)+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[r_{0}(X)+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\varsigma}\log(\bar{\delta}_{n})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)+\frac{16}{\varsigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))\right]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

fourthly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[-\log r_{0}(X)+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[-\log r_{0}(X)+\log\bar{\delta}_{n}]r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\log\bar{\delta}_{n})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\log\bar{\delta}_{n})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\log\bar{\delta}_{n})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

fifthly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+r_{0}(X)]r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\varsigma}\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)+\frac{16}{\varsigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

and lastly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+1]\mathbb{E}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2).

Here, we have applied the inequalities
a\leq\exp(a)
and
\mathds{1}(a>0)\leq\exp(a)
for
a\in\mathbb{R}
, as well as that
-a\log a\leq 1
for
a\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Consequently, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\right\}+c_{1}(1+\log\bar{\delta}_{n})\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

where
c_{1}
is a constant which depends only on
\varsigma,\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))\right\}
and
\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t}))]
. Recall that
\bar{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{1+\kappa}
with
\kappa\in(0,1]
. Setting
\iota_{n}
to
(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1
yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[J(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})-2J_{n}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+J(r_{0})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\right\}+c_{1}(1+\log n)n^{-1}.

Then, we proceed to verify the conditions in Theorem
A.6
. Notice that

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}},T\in\mathbb{R}^{2d}}|g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)|

\displaystyle\leq 3[\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+\log(\iota_{n}+1)]

\displaystyle\leq c_{2}(1+\log n),

where
c_{2}
is a constant which depends only on
\varsigma
and
\kappa
. Furthermore, for any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
,

\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)]=\mathbb{E}_{P}[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})].

The smoothness of
\varphi_{\mathrm{LR}}
then implies that for sufficiently large
n
such that
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
, we have

\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)]\geq\frac{1}{\bar{\delta}_{n}(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[r_{0}(X)-f(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}.

Hence, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(f(X)+1)-\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log r_{0}(X)-\log f(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{Q}\left\{[\log(f(X)+1)-\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(f(X)+1)-\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log r_{0}(X)-\log f(X)]^{2}r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[\log(f(X)+1)-\log(r_{0}(X)+1)]^{2}r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3(1+\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\iota_{n}+\iota_{n})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[r_{0}(X)-f(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 18(\log n)^{5+5\kappa}\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)],

provided that
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
. Therefore, Theorem
A.6
suggests that, for sufficiently large
n
such that
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
, with
n\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
, and for arbitrary
t>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\geq t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{t}{2}+\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left(\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}:\mathbb{E}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T_{i})\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{t}{2}+\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(f,T)]\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 14\mathcal{N}_{n}\left(c_{3}t,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g_{\iota_{n}}(f,\cdot):\mathcal{X}^{t}\times\mathcal{X}^{s}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{nt}{c_{4}(\log n)^{5+5\kappa}}\right),

where
c_{3},c_{4}
are universal constants and
\mathcal{X}^{s},\mathcal{X}^{t}
represents the domain of
X^{s},X^{t}
, respectively. Subsequently, we bound the covering number. Fix
\{x^{s}_{1},\dots,x^{s}_{n}\}\subset(\mathcal{X}^{s})^{n}
and
\{x^{t}_{1},\dots,x^{t}_{n}\}\subset(\mathcal{X}^{t})^{n}
. Let
\mathcal{C}=\{x^{s}_{1},\dots,x^{s}_{n},x^{t}_{1},\dots,x^{t}_{n}\}
, and let
h^{\sharp}=\{h_{1},\dots,h_{k}\}
be an
\epsilon
-covering set of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}|\mathcal{C}}
where
h_{i}=f_{i|\mathcal{C}}
for some
f_{i}\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}(i=1,\dots,k)
, such that for any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
, there exists
h^{*}=f^{*}_{|\mathcal{C}}\in h^{\sharp}
satisfying
\|h^{*}-f_{|\mathcal{C}}\|_{\infty}<\epsilon
. This indicates

\displaystyle|g_{\iota_{n}}(f,(x^{s}_{i},x^{t}_{i}))-g_{\iota_{n}}(f^{*},(x^{s}_{i},x^{t}_{i}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|f(x^{s}_{i})-f^{*}(x^{s}_{i})|+\bar{\delta}_{n}|f(x^{t}_{i})-f^{*}(x^{t}_{i})|+|f(x^{t}_{i})-f^{*}(x^{t}_{i})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\bar{\delta}_{n}+2)\epsilon.

Therefore,

\mathcal{N}_{n}(c_{3}t,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g_{\iota_{n}}(f,\cdot):\mathcal{X}^{t}\times\mathcal{X}^{s}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\})\leq\mathcal{N}_{2n}(c_{3}t/(\bar{\delta}_{n}+2),\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}).

Then, with Lemma
A.4
and Lemma
A.5
, for sufficiently large
n
with
n\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
and any
a_{n}\geq 1/n
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{n}+14\int_{a_{n}}^{\infty}\mathcal{N}_{2n}(c_{3}t/(\bar{\delta}_{n}+2),\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})\exp\left(-\frac{nt}{c_{4}(\log n)^{5+5\kappa}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{n}+14\mathcal{N}_{2n}(c_{3}a_{n}/(\bar{\delta}_{n}+2),\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})\int_{a_{n}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{nt}{c_{4}(\log n)^{5+5\kappa}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{n}+14\left(c_{5}n^{2}\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\right)^{c_{6}SL\log S}\cdot\frac{c_{4}(\log n)^{5+5\kappa}}{n}\exp\left(-\frac{na_{n}}{c_{4}(\log n)^{5+5\kappa}}\right),

where
c_{5}
and
c_{6}
are universal constants. Choose

a_{n}=\frac{c_{4}c_{6}(\log n)^{5+5\kappa}}{n}SL\log S\log\left(c_{5}n^{2}\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\right).

For sufficiently large
n
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T)]-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{\iota_{n}}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}},T_{i})\right\}\leq\frac{c_{7}SL\log S(\log n)^{6+5\kappa}}{n},

where
c_{7}
is a constant not depending on
S,L
and
n
. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.6
.

Assume that

(i)

r_{0}(x)\in\mathcal{H}^{\beta_{r}}_{\mathrm{Loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{d},B_{u})
with
\beta_{r}>0
and
B_{u}\leq c(u^{m}+1)
for some universal constants
c>0
,
m\geq 0
;

(ii)

r_{0}(X^{s})
,
r_{0}(X^{s})^{-1}\mathds{1}(X^{s}\in\mathcal{X}^{t})
and
\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}
are sub-exponentially distributed random variables.

Suppose that the depth
L
and width
M
of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
are expressed as

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=21(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}S_{1}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{1})\rceil+2d+3,

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle=38(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1}S_{2}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{2})\rceil,

for any
S_{1},S_{2}\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. Let
\bar{\delta}=\bar{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{1+\kappa}
and
\underline{\delta}=\underline{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{-1-\kappa}
for arbitrarily fixed
\kappa\in(0,1]
. Then, for sufficiently large
n
, it follows that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c^{*}\left\{\left[(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}(\log n)^{m}\right]^{2}(\log n)^{1+\kappa}+\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n}\right\},

where
c^{*}
is a constant not depending on
S_{1},S_{2}
and
n
.

For any
\iota_{n}\geq 1
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right].

On the one hand, for
n\geq 3
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big\{r_{0}(X)\log r_{0}(X)-[r_{0}(X)+1]\log(r_{0}(X)+1)+\log(f(X)+1)

\displaystyle-r_{0}(X)\log f(X)+r_{0}(X)\log(f(X)+1)\big\}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg(\big\{r_{0}(X)\log r_{0}(X)-[r_{0}(X)+1]\log(r_{0}(X)+1)+\log(f(X)+1)

\displaystyle-r_{0}(X)\log f(X)+r_{0}(X)\log(f(X)+1)\big\}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\left[1+2r_{0}(X)+2r_{0}(X)^{2}+\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)+2r_{0}(X)\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\right]\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Bigg\{\Big[1+\frac{4}{\varsigma}\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)+\frac{32}{\varsigma^{2}}\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)+\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\varsigma}\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\Big]\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)\Bigg\}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}\left[1+\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\right]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

where
c_{1}
is a constant which depends only on
\varsigma
and
\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]
. On the other hand, we first notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{[\log(f(X)+1)-r_{0}(X)\log f(X)+r_{0}(X)\log(f(X)+1)]

\displaystyle\cdot\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{n})\big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle+3\iota_{n}\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle+3\iota_{n}\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\bar{\delta}_{n}\vee\iota_{n})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[r_{0}(X)-f(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\iota_{n}\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2).

Then, we focus on the region
\{x:\|x\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n}\}=[-\iota_{n},\iota_{n}]^{d}
. Let
r_{0}^{*}(x)=r_{0}(2\iota_{n}x-\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d})
for
x\in[0,1]^{d}
. Lemma
A.2
demonstrates that for any
S_{1},S_{2}\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, there exists a function
f^{*}
implemented by a ReLU network with depth
L^{*}=21(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}S_{1}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{1})\rceil+2d
, width
M^{*}=38(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1}S_{2}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{2})\rceil
, such that

|f^{*}(x)-r_{0}^{*}(x)|\leq 18c(\iota_{n}^{m}+1)(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d},

for all
x\in[0,1]^{d}\backslash\Omega([0,1]^{d},K,\Delta)
. Here,

\Omega([0,1]^{d},K,\Delta)=\bigcup_{i=1}^{d}\left\{x=(x_{1},\dots,x_{d})^{\top}:x_{i}\in\bigcup_{k=1}^{K-1}(k/K-\Delta,k/K)\right\},

where
K=\lceil(S_{1}S_{2})^{2/d}\rceil
and
\Delta
is an arbitrary scalar in
(0,1/(3K)]
. Let
f^{\dagger}(x)=f^{*}((x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d})/(2\iota_{n}))
for
x\in[-\iota_{n},\iota_{n}]^{d}
. We obtain that

|f^{\dagger}(x)-r_{0}(x)|\leq 18c(\iota_{n}^{m}+1)(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d},

for all
x\in[-\iota_{n},\iota_{n}]^{d}\backslash\Omega^{\dagger}
, where
\Omega^{\dagger}=\{x:(x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d})/(2\iota_{n})\in\Omega([0,1]^{d},K,\Delta)\}
. Furthermore, note that

f^{\dagger}(x)=f^{*}\left(\frac{x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d}}{2\iota_{n}}\right)=f^{*}\left(\mathrm{relu}\left(\frac{x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d}}{2\iota_{n}}\right)-\mathrm{relu}\left(-\frac{x+\iota_{n}\mathrm{1}_{d}}{2\iota_{n}}\right)\right),

which is implemented by a neural network with ReLU activations, depth
L^{\dagger}=L^{*}+1
, and width
M^{\dagger}=M^{*}
. In addition, let

f^{\ddagger}(x)=\begin{cases}\bar{\delta}_{n},&f^{\dagger}(x)>\bar{\delta}_{n},\\
f^{\dagger}(x),&\underline{\delta}_{n}\leq f^{\dagger}(x)\leq\bar{\delta}_{n},\\
\underline{\delta}_{n},&f^{\dagger}(x)<\underline{\delta}_{n}.\end{cases}

A straightforward calculation shows that

f^{\ddagger}(x)=\mathrm{relu}(-\mathrm{relu}(-f^{\dagger}(x)+\bar{\delta}_{n})+\bar{\delta}_{n}-\underline{\delta}_{n})+\underline{\delta}_{n},

indicating that
f^{\ddagger}(x)
can be implemented by a ReLU network with depth
L=L^{*}+3
and width
M=M^{*}
. Due to the arbitrariness of
\Delta
, when
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[r_{0}(X)-f(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[r_{0}(X)-f^{\ddagger}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[18c(\iota_{n}^{m}+1)(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}\right]^{2}.

Recall that
\bar{\delta}_{n}=(\log n)^{1+\kappa}
, and set
\iota_{n}
to
(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1
. We conclude that for sufficiently large
n
satisfying
\bar{\delta}_{n}\geq\iota_{n}
and
\log n\geq\varsigma/2
, it holds that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X)\|f(X))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\bar{\delta}_{n}\vee\iota_{n})\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[r_{0}(X)-f(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X)\leq\iota_{n})\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+c_{2}\iota_{n}\log(\bar{\delta}_{n}+1)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{3}\left\{\left[(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}(\log n)^{m}\right]^{2}(\log n)^{1+\kappa}+\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n}\right\},

where
c_{2},c_{3}
are constants not depending on
S_{1},S_{2}
and
n
.
∎

To commence, we notice that Lemmas
B.4
,
B.5
and
B.6
indicate

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}SL\log S(\log n)^{6+5\kappa}}{n}

\displaystyle+c_{2}\left\{\left[(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+(\beta_{r}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{r}/d}(\log n)^{m}\right]^{2}(\log n)^{1+\kappa}+\frac{(\log n)^{2}}{n}\right\},

where
c_{1},c_{2}
are constants not depending on
S,L,S_{1},S_{2}
and
n
, and
S_{1},S_{2}
satisfy the conditions that the network depth
L=21(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}S_{1}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{1})\rceil+2d+3
, network width
M=38(\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1)^{2}d^{\lfloor\beta_{r}\rfloor+1}S_{2}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{2})\rceil
, for sufficiently large
n
and
n\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
. Therefore, by letting
S_{1}=\mathcal{O}(n^{d/(2d+4\beta_{r})})
and
S_{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, we obtain

M=\mathcal{O}(1),\quad L=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{\frac{d}{2d+4\beta_{r}}}\log n\right),\quad S=\mathcal{O}(M^{2}L)=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{\frac{d}{2d+4\beta_{r}}}\log n\right),

yielding

\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s}))\leq c_{3}n^{-\frac{2\beta_{r}}{d+2\beta_{r}}}(\log n)^{(9+5\kappa)\vee(2m+1+\kappa)},

where
c_{3}
is a constant not depending on
n
, for
n\geq 3
.
Furthermore, note that for any
\iota_{n}\geq 1
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})-\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[r_{0}(X^{s})-\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X^{s})\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[r_{0}(X^{s})-\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\mathds{1}(X^{s}\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[r_{0}(X^{s})-\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right\}.

Specifically, on one hand, the smoothness of
\varphi_{\mathrm{LR}}
demonstrates that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[r_{0}(X^{s})-\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X^{s})\leq\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\bar{\delta}_{n}\vee\iota_{n})[(\bar{\delta}_{n}\vee\iota_{n})+1]\mathbb{E}\left[D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s}))\mathds{1}(\iota_{n}^{-1}\leq r_{0}(X^{s})\leq\iota_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\bar{\delta}_{n}\vee\iota_{n})[(\bar{\delta}_{n}\vee\iota_{n})+1]\mathbb{E}D_{\mathrm{LR}}(r_{0}(X^{s})\|\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})).

On the other hand, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[r_{0}(X^{s})-\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\mathds{1}(X^{s}\in\mathcal{X}^{t})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(1+\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})<\iota_{n}^{-1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(1+\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2})\mathbb{E}_{P}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(1+\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2})\mathbb{E}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)^{-1}\mathds{1}(X\in\mathcal{X}^{t})/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[r_{0}(X^{s})-\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})\right]+2\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\iota_{n})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{32}{\varsigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2)+2\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X)/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/2).

Hence, by letting
\iota_{n}=(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1
, we have for
n\geq 3
,

\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})\leq c_{4}n^{-\frac{2\beta_{r}}{d+2\beta_{r}}}(\log n)^{(11+7\kappa)\vee(2m+3+3\kappa)},

where
c_{4}
is a constant not depending on
n
. In addition, we note that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{t}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{t})-r_{0}(X^{t})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\right\}.

Similar truncation operation suggests that, for any
\varrho_{n}>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{t}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})\leq\varrho_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\varrho_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varrho_{n}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\varrho_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\varrho_{n}\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\varrho_{n})\right\}.

Subsequently, note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}}(X^{s})-r_{0}(X^{s})\right]^{2}r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X^{s})>\varrho_{n})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[r_{0}(X)\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\varrho_{n})\right]+2\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[r_{0}(X)^{3}\mathds{1}(r_{0}(X)>\varrho_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{\varsigma}\bar{\delta}_{n}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\exp(-\varsigma\varrho_{n}/2)+\frac{432}{\varsigma^{3}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]\exp(-\varsigma\varrho_{n}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{5}[(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}+1]\exp(-\varsigma\varrho_{n}/2),

where
c_{5}
is a constant which depends only on
\varsigma
and
\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma r_{0}(X))]
. Hence, by taking
\varrho_{n}=(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1
, we obtain that

\mathcal{R}^{t}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})\leq[(2\varsigma^{-1}\log n)\vee 1]\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{r}_{\mathrm{LR}})+\frac{c_{5}[(\log n)^{2+2\kappa}+1]}{n}.

This completes the proof.
∎

B.4
Proof of Lemma 4.1

For any
\iota_{n}>0
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}|V|\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}|V|\mathds{1}(|V|\leq\iota_{n})\right)+\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}|V|\mathds{1}(|V|>\iota_{n})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\iota_{n}\gamma_{n}+\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}|V|\mathds{1}(|V|>\iota_{n})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\iota_{n}\gamma_{n}+4\varsigma^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}\exp(\varsigma|V|/4)\mathds{1}(|V|>\iota_{n})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\iota_{n}\gamma_{n}+4\varsigma^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}\exp(\varsigma|V|/2)\right)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/4),

where we have applied the inequalities that
a\leq\exp(a)
and
\mathds{1}(a>0)\leq\exp(a)
. Let
U_{n,(j)}
and
U_{(j)}
be the
j
-th entry of
U_{n}
and
U
, respectively, for
j=1,\dots,d
. Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}\exp(\varsigma|V|/2)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(U_{n,(j)}-U_{(j)}\right)^{2}\exp(\varsigma|V|/2)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(U_{n,(j)}-U_{(j)}\right)^{4}\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma|V|)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left[8\mathbb{E}\left(U_{n,(j)}^{4}+U_{(j)}^{4}\right)\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma|V|)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq d\left[8\left(\xi_{n}^{4}+\mathbb{E}\|U\|_{\infty}^{4}\right)\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma|V|)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}d(\xi_{n}^{2}+1),

where
c_{3}
is a constant only depending on
\mathbb{E}\|U\|_{\infty}^{4}
and
\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma|V|)
. Therefore, we have

\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}|V|\right)\leq\iota_{n}\gamma_{n}+4c_{3}\varsigma^{-1}d(\xi_{n}^{2}+1)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{n}/4).

Let
\iota_{n}=4\varsigma^{-1}\log n
. Then, for
n\geq 2
, it follows that

\mathbb{E}\left(\|U_{n}-U\|_{2}^{2}|V|\right)\leq 4\varsigma^{-1}\gamma_{n}\log n+\frac{4c_{3}\varsigma^{-1}d(\xi_{n}^{2}+1)}{n}.

This completes the proof.
∎

B.5
Proof of Proposition 4.3

For any
\iota_{N}>0
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{t})-\theta_{0}(X^{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle G(\iota_{N})\mathbb{E}\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right].

Let
\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})
and
\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})
be the
j
-th component of
\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})
and
\theta_{0}(X^{s})
, respectively, for
j=1,\dots,k
. By using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality twice, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}/4)\right\}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\left(\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{4}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}/2)\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\left(\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{8}\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty})\right\}^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle k\left(\left\{128\left(\xi_{N}^{8}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|^{8}\right)\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty})\right\}^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{3}k\left(\xi_{N}^{2}+1\right)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4),

where
c_{3}
is a constant only depending on
\mathbb{E}\|\theta_{0}(X^{s})\|^{8}
,
\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty})
and
\mathbb{E}[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}]
. Hence, let
\iota_{N}=4\varsigma^{-1}\log N
and we obtain the result.
∎

B.6
Proof of Proposition 4.4

For any
\iota_{N}>0
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{t})-\theta_{0}(X^{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle G(\iota_{N})\mathbb{E}\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right].

Let
\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})
and
\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})
be the
j
-th component of
\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})
and
\theta_{0}(X^{s})
, respectively, for
j=1,\dots,k
. By using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality twice, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{\theta}_{N}(X^{s})-\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\mathds{1}(\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{2}\cdot r_{0}(X^{s})\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}^{2}/4)\right\}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}^{2}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\left(\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{4}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}^{2}/2)\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}^{2}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{k}\left(\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\theta}_{N,(j)}(X^{s})-\theta_{0,(j)}(X^{s})\right]_{2}^{8}\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}^{2})\right\}^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}^{2}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle k\left(\left\{128\left(\xi_{N}^{8}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\theta_{0}(X^{s})\right\|^{8}\right)\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}^{2})\right\}^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}^{2}/4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{3}k\left(\xi_{N}^{2}+1\right)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}^{2}/4),

where
c_{3}
is a constant only depending on
\mathbb{E}\|\theta_{0}(X^{s})\|^{8}
,
\mathbb{E}\exp(\varsigma\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}^{2})
and
\mathbb{E}[r_{0}(X^{s})^{2}]
. Hence, let
\iota_{N}=2(\varsigma^{-1}\log N)^{1/2}
and we obtain the result.
∎

B.7
Proof of Theorem 5.1

For any
X^{s}
-square-integrable function
f:\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\to\mathbb{R}^{d_{y}}
such that
\mathbb{E}\|f(X^{s})\|_{2}^{2}<\infty
, define

\displaystyle K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}\|Y-f(X)\|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(f)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|Y^{s}_{i}-f(X^{s}_{i})\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Here,
\mathbb{E}_{P}[h(X,Y)]\equiv\mathbb{E}[h(X^{s},Y^{s})]
for any
(X^{s},Y^{s})
-integrable function
h
, where the expectation is taken with respect to
(X^{s},Y^{s})
.

Lemma B.7
.

Assume that
\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}
and
\|f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{\infty}
attain a finite second moment. Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\hat{f}^{s}_{N}(X^{s})-f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]+2\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}.

Given that
\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}
and
\|f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{\infty}
have a finite second moment, we have
\mathbb{E}\|Y^{s}\|_{2}^{2}<\infty
and
\mathbb{E}\|f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{2}^{2}<\infty
. For any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\hat{f}^{s}_{N}(X^{s})-f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]+2\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(f)-K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]+2\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(f)-K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(f_{0})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(f_{0})-K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]+2[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f)-K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]+2\mathbb{E}_{P}\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}.

This indicates that
\mathcal{R}^{s}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})\leq\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]+2\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}
.
∎

Lemma B.8
.

Assume that
\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}
is sub-exponentially distributed. Let
\bar{\delta}=\bar{\delta}_{N}=(\log N)^{1+\kappa}
, with an arbitrarily fixed
\kappa\in(0,1]
, and let
\underline{\delta}=\underline{\delta}_{N}=-(\log N)^{1+\kappa}
. Then, for sufficiently large
N
and
N\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
, it follows that

\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]\leq\frac{c^{*}SL\log S(\log N)^{5+4\kappa}}{N},

where
c^{*}
is a constant not depending on
S,L
and
N
.

Let
T_{i}=(X^{s}_{i},Y^{s}_{i})
for
i=1,\dots,N
,
\mathcal{D}_{N}=\{T_{1},\dots,T_{N}\}
, and
T=(X^{s},Y^{s})
be an independent copy of
T_{1}
. Firstly, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\{K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})-2[K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(f_{0})]\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g(\hat{f}^{s}_{N},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g(\hat{f}^{s}_{N},T_{i})\right\},

where
g(f,T)=\|Y^{s}-f(X^{s})\|_{2}^{2}-\|Y^{s}-f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{2}^{2}
for
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}}
. For a
d_{y}
-dimensional vector
v
, denote its
j
-th component as
v_{(j)}
; additionally, we denote the
j
-th output coordinate of a function
f:\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\to\mathbb{R}^{d_{y}}
as
f_{(j)}
, with
j\in\{1,\dots,d_{y}\}
. Furthermore, for any measurable function
h:\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\to\mathbb{R}
, define

g_{j}(h,T)=[Y^{s}_{(j)}-h(X^{s})]^{2}-[Y^{s}_{(j)}-f_{0}(X^{s})_{(j)}]^{2},\quad\text{for }j=1,\dots,d_{y}.

It is then clear that
g(f,T)=\sum_{j=1}^{d_{y}}g_{j}(f_{(j)},T)
. Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[K^{\mathrm{reg}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})-2K^{\mathrm{reg}}_{N}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N})+K^{\mathrm{reg}}(f_{0})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g(\hat{f}^{s}_{N},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g(\hat{f}^{s}_{N},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d_{y}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}.

Subsequently, let us fix an arbitrary
j\in\{1,\dots,d_{y}\}
. For any
\iota_{N}>0
, we let
U=Y^{s}_{(j)}\mathds{1}(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{N})
and
V=\mathbb{E}[Y^{s}_{(j)}\mathds{1}(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{N})|X^{s}]
. Then, for any measurable function
h:\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\to\mathbb{R}
, define

g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h,T)=[U-h(X^{s})]^{2}-(U-V)^{2}=[V-h(X^{s})][2U-h(X^{s})-V].

It follows that

\displaystyle|g_{j}(f_{(j)},T)-g_{j,\iota_{N}}(f_{(j)},T)|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|[Y^{s}_{(j)}-f(X^{s})_{(j)}]^{2}-[U-f(X^{s})_{(j)}]^{2}-[Y^{s}_{(j)}-f_{0}(X^{s})_{(j)}]^{2}+(U-V)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|(Y^{s}_{(j)}-U)[Y^{s}_{(j)}+U-2f(X^{s})_{(j)}]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|[Y^{s}_{(j)}-U-f_{0}(X^{s})_{(j)}+V][Y^{s}_{(j)}+U-f_{0}(X^{s})_{(j)}-V]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|Y^{s}_{(j)}\mathds{1}(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})[Y^{s}_{(j)}+U-2f(X^{s})_{(j)}]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|Y^{s}_{(j)}\mathds{1}(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})[Y^{s}_{(j)}+U-f_{0}(X^{s})_{(j)}-V]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}[Y^{s}_{(j)}\mathds{1}(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})|X^{s}][Y^{s}_{(j)}+U-f_{0}(X^{s})_{(j)}-V]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}+\|f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{\infty}+3\iota_{N}+2\bar{\delta}_{N}\right)\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})

\displaystyle+\left(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}+\|f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{\infty}+2\iota_{N}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})|X^{s}\right].

Taking expectation, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left(2\|Y\|_{\infty}+\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}+3\iota_{N}+2\bar{\delta}_{N}\right)\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle+3\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\left(\|Y\|_{\infty}+\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}+2\iota_{N}\right)\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})|X\right]\right\}.

Specifically, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\left(2\|Y\|_{\infty}+\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}+3\iota_{N}+2\bar{\delta}_{N}\right)\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|Y\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle+(3\iota_{N}+2\bar{\delta}_{N})\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{32}{\varsigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/2)+\frac{4}{\varsigma}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\varsigma}(3\iota_{N}+2\bar{\delta}_{N})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/2)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{32}{\varsigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/2)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\varsigma}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{P}(\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}^{2})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\right\}^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\varsigma}(3\iota_{N}+2\bar{\delta}_{N})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/2),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\left(\|Y\|_{\infty}+\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}+2\iota_{N}\right)\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})|X\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})|X\right]\right\}+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle+2\iota_{N}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\mathbb{E}_{P}(\|Y\|_{\infty}^{2})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\|Y\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})]\right\}^{1/2}+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle+2\iota_{N}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left[\|Y\|_{\infty}\mathds{1}(\|Y\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{\varsigma}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{P}(\|Y\|_{\infty}^{2})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\right\}^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\varsigma}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{P}(\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}^{2})\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\right\}^{1/2}\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\varsigma}\iota_{N}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/2).

Here, we have applied the inequalities
a\leq\exp(a)
and
\mathds{1}(a>0)\leq\exp(a)
for
a\in\mathbb{R}
. Note that
\mathbb{E}_{P}\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}^{2}<\infty
since
\|Y^{s}\|_{\infty}
has a finite second moment and
f_{0}(X^{s})=\mathbb{E}(Y^{s}|X^{s})
. As a consequence, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}+c_{1}(\iota_{N}+\bar{\delta}_{N}+1)\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/4),

where
c_{1}
is a constant which depends only on
\varsigma
and
\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|Y\|_{\infty})]
. Recall that
\bar{\delta}_{N}=(\log N)^{1+\kappa}
. Set
\iota_{N}
to
(4\varsigma^{-1}\log N)\vee 1
. Then, for sufficiently large
N
such that
\bar{\delta}_{N}\geq\iota_{N}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}+3c_{1}(\log N)^{1+\kappa}N^{-1}.

Furthermore, we proceed to verify the conditions in Theorem
A.6
. Notice that

\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}},T\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+d_{y}}}|g_{j,\iota_{N}}(f_{(j)},T)|\leq 6\iota_{N}^{2}+2\bar{\delta}_{N}^{2}\leq 8\bar{\delta}_{N}^{2}=8(\log N)^{2+2\kappa},

whenever
\bar{\delta}_{N}\geq\iota_{N}
. Furthermore, for any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(f_{(j)},T)]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{[V-f(X^{s})_{(j)}][2U-f(X^{s})_{(j)}-V]\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[V-f(X^{s})_{(j)}]^{2},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(f,T)^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{[V-f(X^{s})_{(j)}]^{2}[2U-f(X^{s})_{(j)}-V]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq(3\iota_{N}+\bar{\delta}_{N})^{2}\mathbb{E}[V-f(X^{s})_{(j)}]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 16\bar{\delta}_{N}^{2}\mathbb{E}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(f_{(j)},T)]

\displaystyle=16(\log N)^{2+2\kappa}\mathbb{E}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(f_{(j)},T)],

provided that
N
is sufficiently large such that
\bar{\delta}_{N}\geq\iota_{N}
. Hence, Theorem
A.6
suggests that, for sufficiently large
N
such that
\bar{\delta}_{N}\geq\iota_{N}
, with
N\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
, and for arbitrary
t>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\geq t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{t}{2}+\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left(\exists h\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}:\mathbb{E}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h,T)]-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h,T_{i})\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{t}{2}+\frac{t}{2}+\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h,T)]\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 14\mathcal{N}_{N}\left(c_{2}t,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h,\cdot):\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\times\mathbb{R}^{d_{y}}\to\mathbb{R},h\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{Nt}{c_{3}(\log N)^{4+4\kappa}}\right),

where
c_{2},c_{3}
are universal constants. Subsequently, we bound the covering number. Fix
\{x_{1},\dots,x_{N}\}\subset(\mathcal{R}^{d_{x}})^{N}
and
\{y_{1},\dots,y_{N}\}\subset(\mathcal{R}^{d_{y}})^{N}
. Let
\mathcal{C}=\{x_{1},\dots,x_{N}\}
, and let
w^{\sharp}=\{w_{1},\dots,w_{k}\}
be an
\epsilon
-covering set of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}|\mathcal{C}}
where
w_{i}=h_{i|\mathcal{C}}
for some
h_{i}\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\ (i=1,\dots,k)
, such that for any
h\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}
, there exists
w^{*}=h^{*}_{|\mathcal{C}}\in w^{\sharp}
satisfying
\|w^{*}-h_{|\mathcal{C}}\|_{\infty}<\epsilon
. This indicates

\displaystyle|g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h,(x_{i},y_{i}))-g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h^{*},(x_{i},y_{i}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|h^{*}(x_{i})-h(x_{i})|\cdot\left|2y_{i,(j)}\mathds{1}(\|y_{i}\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{N})-h^{*}(x_{i})-h(x_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\iota_{N}+\bar{\delta}_{N})\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\bar{\delta}_{N}\epsilon,

whenever
\bar{\delta}_{N}\geq\iota_{N}
. Therefore,

\mathcal{N}_{N}(c_{2}t,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g_{j,\iota_{N}}(h,\cdot):\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\times\mathbb{R}^{d_{y}}\to\mathbb{R},h\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}\})\leq\mathcal{N}_{N}(c_{2}t/(4\bar{\delta}_{N}),\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}).

Then, with Lemma
A.4
and Lemma
A.5
, for sufficiently large
N
with
N\geq\mathrm{Pdim}(\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})
and any
a_{N}\geq 1/N
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{N}+14\int_{a_{N}}^{\infty}\mathcal{N}_{N}(c_{2}t/(4\bar{\delta}_{N}),\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})\exp\left(-\frac{Nt}{c_{3}(\log N)^{4+4\kappa}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{N}+14\mathcal{N}_{N}(c_{2}a_{N}/(4\bar{\delta}_{N}),\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}})\int_{a_{N}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{Nt}{c_{3}(\log N)^{4+4\kappa}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle a_{N}+14\left(c_{4}N^{2}\bar{\delta}_{N}^{2}\right)^{c_{5}SL\log S}\cdot\frac{c_{3}(\log N)^{4+4\kappa}}{N}\exp\left(-\frac{Na_{N}}{c_{3}(\log N)^{4+4\kappa}}\right),

where
c_{4}
and
c_{5}
are universal constants. Choose

a_{N}=\frac{c_{3}c_{5}(\log N)^{4+4\kappa}}{N}SL\log S\log\left(c_{4}N^{2}\bar{\delta}_{N}^{2}\right).

For sufficiently large
N
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{D}_{N}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T}[g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T)]-\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{j,\iota_{N}}(\hat{f}^{s}_{N,(j)},T_{i})\right\}\leq\frac{c_{6}SL\log S(\log N)^{5+4\kappa}}{N},

where
c_{6}
is a constant not depending on
S,L
and
N
. Noticing the arbitrariness of
j
, we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma B.9
.

Assume that

(i)

e_{j}^{\top}f_{0}\in\mathcal{H}^{\beta_{f}}_{\mathrm{Loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{d},B_{u})
with
\beta_{f}>0
and
B_{u}\leq c(u^{m}+1)
for some universal constants
c>0
,
m\geq 0
, and for any
j\in\{1,\dots,d_{y}\}
, where
e_{j}
denotes a
d_{y}
-dimensional one-hot vector with the
j
-th component equal to 1 and all other components equal to 0;

(ii)

\|f_{0}(X^{s})\|_{\infty}
and
\|X^{s}\|_{\infty}
are sub-exponentially distributed random variables.

Suppose that the depth
L
and width
M
of
\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}}
are expressed as

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=21(\lfloor\beta_{f}\rfloor+1)^{2}S_{1}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{1})\rceil+2d_{x}+3,

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle=38d_{y}(\lfloor\beta_{f}\rfloor+1)^{2}d_{x}^{\lfloor\beta_{f}\rfloor+1}S_{2}\lceil\log_{2}(8S_{2})\rceil,

for any
S_{1},S_{2}\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. Let
\bar{\delta}=\bar{\delta}_{N}=(\log N)^{1+\kappa}
, with an arbitrarily fixed
\kappa\in(0,1]
, and let
\underline{\delta}=\underline{\delta}_{N}=-(\log N)^{-1-\kappa}
. Then, for sufficiently large
N
, it follows that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathrm{NN}}^{d_{y}}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c^{*}\left\{\left[(\lfloor\beta_{f}\rfloor+1)^{2}d_{x}^{\lfloor\beta_{f}\rfloor+(\beta_{f}\vee 1)/2}(S_{1}S_{2})^{-2\beta_{f}/d_{x}}(\log N)^{m}\right]^{2}+\frac{(\log N)^{2+2\kappa}}{N}\right\},

where
c^{*}
is a constant not depending on
S_{1},S_{2}
and
N
.

For any
\iota_{N}>0
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}\leq\iota_{N})\right\}+\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right\}.

For clarity, denote the
j
-th output coordinate of a function
f:\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\to\mathbb{R}^{d_{y}}
as
f_{(j)}
, with
j\in\{1,\dots,d_{y}\}
. On the one hand, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{\|f(X)-f_{0}(X)\|_{2}^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d_{y}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\left\{[f_{(j)}(X)-f_{0,(j)}(X)]^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d_{y}\bar{\delta}_{N}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})]+2d_{y}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathds{1}(\|X\|_{\infty}>\iota_{N})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d_{y}\bar{\delta}_{N}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/2)

\displaystyle+2d_{y}\mathbb{E}_{P}[\|f_{0}(X)\|_{\infty}^{2}\exp(\varsigma\|X\|_{\infty}/2)]\exp(-\varsigma\iota_{N}/2)

\displaystyle\leq
