Step 5. Leveraging the Bernstein’s inequality. Firstly, we note that

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{Var}[U_{i}h(z_{i})]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}h(z_{i})^{2}\mathrm{Var}(U_{i})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}h(z_{i})^{2}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}h(z_{i})\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\alpha}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}+\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}h(z_{i})^{2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V_{i}\right|\geq A_{1}+A_{2}\sigma^{2}\right),

where

\displaystyle V_{i}

\displaystyle=U_{i}h(z_{i}),\quad\sigma^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{Var}[U_{i}h(z_{i})]

\displaystyle A_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\alpha}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)},\quad A_{2}=\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}.

Observe that
V_{1},\dots,V_{n}
are independent random variables satisfying
|V_{i}|\leq|h(z_{i})|\leq\xi_{n}(i=1,\dots,n)
, and that
A_{1},A_{2}>0
for
n\geq N
. By Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V_{i}\right|\geq A_{1}+A_{2}\sigma^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{n(A_{1}+A_{2}\sigma^{2})^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}+2(A_{1}+A_{2}\sigma^{2})\frac{\xi_{n}}{3}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\exp\left(-\frac{nA_{2}^{2}}{\frac{2}{3}\xi_{n}A_{2}}\cdot\frac{(\frac{A_{1}}{A_{2}}+\sigma^{2})^{2}}{\frac{A_{1}}{A_{2}}+(1+\frac{3}{\xi_{n}A_{2}})\sigma^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\exp\left(-\frac{3nA_{2}}{2\xi_{n}}\cdot\frac{(\frac{A_{1}}{A_{2}}+\sigma^{2})^{2}}{\frac{A_{1}}{A_{2}}+(1+\frac{3}{\xi_{n}A_{2}})\sigma^{2}}\right).

It is easy to verify that for arbitrary
a,b,u>0
, it follows that

\frac{(a+u)^{2}}{a+bu}\geq\frac{4a}{b^{2}}[(b-1)\vee 0].

Then, by letting
a=A_{1}/A_{2}
,
b=1+3/(\xi_{n}A_{2}),u=\sigma^{2}
, we obtain

\frac{3nA_{2}}{2\xi_{n}}\cdot\frac{(\frac{A_{1}}{A_{2}}+\sigma^{2})^{2}}{\frac{A_{1}}{A_{2}}+(1+\frac{3}{\xi_{n}A_{2}})\sigma^{2}}\geq\frac{3nA_{2}}{2\xi_{n}}\cdot\frac{4\frac{A_{1}}{A_{2}}}{(1+\frac{3}{\xi_{n}A_{2}})^{2}}\cdot\frac{3}{\xi_{n}A_{2}}=\frac{18nA_{1}A_{2}}{(\xi_{n}A_{2}+3)^{2}}.

In addition, notice that for
n\geq N
and
0<\epsilon\leq 1/2
,

A_{1}=\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\alpha}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}>\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\alpha}{4(1+\epsilon)}=\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{4}\left(1-\frac{\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}\right)\geq\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{6},

which results in

\displaystyle\frac{18nA_{1}A_{2}}{(\xi_{n}A_{2}+3)^{2}}

\displaystyle=18n\cdot\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{6}\cdot\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\cdot\frac{1}{\left[\frac{\xi_{n}\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}+3\right]^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq 18n\cdot\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{6}\cdot\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\cdot\frac{1}{\left(\frac{\gamma}{20}+3\right)^{2}}\geq\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)},

where
\omega=400/(\gamma+60)^{2}
. To conclude, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}h(z_{i})\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\alpha}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}+\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}h(z_{i})^{2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V_{i}\right|\geq A_{1}+A_{2}\sigma^{2}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\right).

Step 6. Conclusion. We have shown that, for
n>(8\zeta_{n}/[\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)])\vee N
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}:\mathbb{E}[g(f,Z)]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(f,Z_{i})\geq\epsilon\left\{\alpha+\beta+\mathbb{E}[g(f,Z)]\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8}{7}\mathbb{P}\left\{\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}:\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(f,Z_{i}^{\prime})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(f,Z_{i})\geq\frac{\epsilon}{2}(\alpha+\beta)+\frac{\epsilon}{2}\mathbb{E}[g(f,Z)]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8}{7}\Bigg(\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}:\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[g(f,Z_{i}^{\prime})-g(f,Z_{i})]\geq\frac{\epsilon}{2}(\alpha+\beta)-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)}{2\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[g(f,Z_{i})^{2}+g(f,Z_{i}^{\prime})^{2}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+8\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{(\alpha+\beta)\epsilon}{5},\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}_{|\mathcal{D}_{n}}\right)\right]\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8}{7}\Bigg(2\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}:\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}g(f,Z_{i})\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon}{4}(\alpha+\beta)-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}+\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(f,Z_{i})^{2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+8\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{(\alpha+\beta)\epsilon}{5},\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}_{|\mathcal{D}_{n}}\right)\right]\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8}{7}\Bigg\{2\mathbb{E}\left(|\mathcal{C}_{\varpi\epsilon\beta}|\max_{h^{\sharp}\in\mathcal{C}_{\varpi\epsilon\beta}}\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}h(Z_{i})\right|\geq\frac{\epsilon\alpha}{4}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\alpha}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}+\frac{\epsilon(1-\epsilon)}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}h(Z_{i})^{2}\Bigg\}\right)

\displaystyle+8\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{(\alpha+\beta)\epsilon}{5},\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}_{|\mathcal{D}_{n}}\right)\right]\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}\right)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8}{7}\Bigg\{2\mathbb{E}\left[2|\mathcal{C}_{\varpi\epsilon\beta}|\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+8\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{(\alpha+\beta)\epsilon}{5},\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}_{|\mathcal{D}_{n}}\right)\right]\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}\right)\Bigg\}.

While conditioning on
Z_{i}=z_{i}
for
i=1,\dots,n
, we choose the
\varpi\epsilon\beta
-covering set of minimal size, which yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}:\mathbb{E}[g(f,Z)]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(f,Z_{i})\geq\epsilon\left\{\alpha+\beta+\mathbb{E}[g(f,Z)]\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{32}{7}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}\left(\varpi\epsilon\beta,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}_{|\mathcal{D}_{n}}\right)\right]\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{64}{7}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}\left(\frac{(\alpha+\beta)\epsilon}{5},\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}_{|\mathcal{D}_{n}}\right)\right]\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{32}{7}\mathcal{N}_{n}\left(\varpi\epsilon\beta,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{64}{7}\mathcal{N}_{n}\left(\frac{(\alpha+\beta)\epsilon}{5},\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}\right).

Observe that for
n\geq N
, we have
\eta\geq 1
,
\gamma>0
,

\varpi=\frac{6\eta-2}{30\eta+3\gamma\eta}\leq\frac{6\eta}{30\eta}=\frac{1}{5},\quad\omega=\frac{400}{(\gamma+60)^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{9},

and

\displaystyle\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{27\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{40\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\geq\frac{27\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{40(\xi_{n}^{2}\vee\zeta_{n})(1+\epsilon)},

\displaystyle\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3\epsilon^{2}\alpha n}{40(\xi_{n}^{2}\vee\zeta_{n})}\geq\frac{27\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{40(\xi_{n}^{2}\vee\zeta_{n})(1+\epsilon)}.

Consequently, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\exists f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}:\mathbb{E}[g(f,Z)]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(f,Z_{i})\geq\epsilon\left\{\alpha+\beta+\mathbb{E}[g(f,Z)]\right\}\right)

(12)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{32}{7}\mathcal{N}_{n}\left(\varpi\epsilon\beta,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{4\zeta_{n}(1+\epsilon)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{64}{7}\mathcal{N}_{n}\left(\frac{(\alpha+\beta)\epsilon}{5},\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{3\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)n}{40\xi_{n}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 14\mathcal{N}_{n}\left(\varpi\epsilon\beta,\|\cdot\|_{\infty},\{g(f,\cdot):\mathcal{Z}\to\mathbb{R},f\in\mathcal{F}_{n}\}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{27\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{40(\xi_{n}^{2}\vee\zeta_{n})(1+\epsilon)}\right),

for
n>(8\zeta_{n}/[\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)])\vee N
. When
N\leq n\leq 8\zeta_{n}/[\epsilon^{2}(\alpha+\beta)])
, on the other hand, we note

\exp\left(-\frac{27\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)\alpha\omega n}{40(\xi_{n}^{2}\vee\zeta_{n})(1+\epsilon)}\right)\geq\exp\left(-\frac{3}{5}\right)\geq\frac{1}{14},

demonstrating that the last right-hand side of Eqn. (
12
) exceeds one, and hence the inequality holds trivially, which completes the proof.
∎
